wet_skv_terrors_A41 = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      exists = yes
      wet_skv_hostile = yes
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_terrors_victim
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_terrors_victim
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_government = democratic
      }
      party_leader = {
        limit = {
          has_ideology = democratic
        }
        set_character_flag = wet_skv_terrors_incumbent
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_government = fascism
      }
      party_leader = {
        limit = {
          has_ideology = fascism
        }
        set_character_flag = wet_skv_terrors_incumbent
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_government = communism
      }
      party_leader = {
        limit = {
          has_ideology = communism
        }
        set_character_flag = wet_skv_terrors_incumbent
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_government = neutrality
      }
      party_leader = {
        limit = {
          has_ideology = neutrality
        }
        set_character_flag = wet_skv_terrors_incumbent
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_government = waaagh
      }
      party_leader = {
        limit = {
          has_ideology = waaagh
        }
        set_character_flag = wet_skv_terrors_incumbent
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_government = horned_rat
      }
      party_leader = {
        limit = {
          has_ideology = horned_rat
        }
        set_character_flag = wet_skv_terrors_incumbent
      }
    }
    kill_country_leader = yes
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_character = WET_A41_commander_marshal
      }
      add_country_leader_role = {
        character = WET_A41_commander_marshal
        promote_leader = yes
        country_leader = {
          ideology = wet_order_myrmidia
          expire = "2902.1.31.1"
          traits = {
            
          }
        }
      }
    }
    else = {
      add_country_leader_role = {
        character = WET_SKV_regency_A41
        promote_leader = yes
        country_leader = {
          ideology = wet_order_myrmidia
          expire = "2902.1.31.1"
          traits = {
            
          }
        }
      }
    }
    add_stability = -0.4
    add_war_support = -0.1
    add_timed_idea = {
      idea = wet_skv_shattered_court
      days = 180
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_skaven.102
    }
  }
}

      }
    }

    A41 = {
      wet_skv_terrors_A41 = yes
    }
    A57 = {
      wet_skv_terrors_A57 = yes
    }
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        exists = yes
        wet_skv_in_old_world = yes
        NOT = {
          tag = U25
        }
        NOT = {
          tag = A41
        }
        NOT = {
          tag = A57
        }
        NOT = {
          tag = WST
        }
      }
      country_event = {
        id = wet_skaven.104
      }
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_skaven.105
    }
  }
}
wet_skv_measure_bloc = {
  clear_array = wet_skv_defender_leaders
  clear_array = wet_skv_defenders
  for_each_scope_loop = {
    array = wet_skv_war_target
    U25 = {
      add_to_array = {
        wet_skv_defender_leaders = PREV
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        is_subject = yes
      }
      overlord = {
        U25 = {
          add_to_array = {
            wet_skv_defender_leaders = PREV
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  set_variable = {
    wet_skv_defending_manpower = 0
  }
  for_each_scope_loop = {
    array = wet_skv_defender_leaders
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        exists = yes
        NOT = {
          tag = U25
        }
        OR = {
          tag = PREV
          is_subject_of = PREV
          is_in_faction_with = PREV
          PREV = {
            is_guaranteed_by = PREV
          }
        }
        NOT = {
          is_in_array = {
            U25.wet_skv_defenders = THIS
          }
        }
      }
      U25 = {
        add_to_array = {
          wet_skv_defenders = PREV
        }
        add_to_variable = {
          var = wet_skv_defending_manpower
          value = PREV.deployed_army_manpower_k
        }
      }
    }
  }
  multiply_variable = {
    wet_skv_defending_manpower = 1.25
  }
  clear_array = wet_skv_defender_leaders
  clear_array = wet_skv_defenders
}

wet_skv_send_service_eshin = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      tag = U28
      exists = yes
      is_subject = no
      NOT = {
        has_war_with = U25
      }
      U25 = {
        exists = yes
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_service_eshin
      }
      check_variable = {
        wet_skv_offer_service_eshin_cooldown
        <
        1
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_service_eshin_accepted
      }
      OR = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_service_eshin_attempted
        }
        U25 = {
          has_political_power
          >
          -1
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_service_eshin_attempted
      }
      U25 = {
        add_political_power = -0
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_service_eshin_attempted
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_service_eshin
    set_variable = {
      wet_skv_offer_service_eshin_days = 30
    }
    U25 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            is_in_array = {
              wet_skv_active_recipients = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        add_to_array = {
          wet_skv_active_recipients = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_skaven.106
    }
  }
}
wet_skv_request_eshin = {
  U28 = {
    wet_skv_send_service_eshin = yes
  }
}

wet_skv_admit_candidate = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_skv_admission_eligible = yes
      has_country_flag = wet_skv_membership_consent
    }
    U25 = {
      faction_leader = {
        add_to_faction = PREV.PREV
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        is_in_faction_with = U25
      }
      U25 = {
        add_political_power = -50
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_country_flag = wet_skv_first_invite_discount
            NOT = {
              has_country_flag = wet_skv_first_invite_paid
            }
          }
          add_political_power = 25
          set_country_flag = wet_skv_first_invite_paid
        }
        add_to_variable = {
          wet_skv_authority = 5
        }
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_skv_membership_consent
    }
  }
}
wet_skv_send_invite = {

    limit = {
      wet_skv_admission_eligible = yes
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_invite

      check_variable = {
        wet_skv_offer_invite_cooldown
        <
        1
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_invite_accepted
      }
      OR = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_invite_attempted
        }
        U25 = {
          has_political_power
          >
          -1
        }
      }

        add_political_power = -0

    set_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_invite
    set_variable = {
      wet_skv_offer_invite_days = 30

    U25 = {

}
wet_skv_send_depot = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      exists = yes
      is_in_faction_with = U25
      NOT = {
        has_war_with = U25
      }
      wet_skv_covert_access = yes
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_depot
      }
      check_variable = {
        wet_skv_offer_depot_cooldown
        <
        1
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_depot_accepted
      }
      OR = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_depot_attempted
        }
        U25 = {
          has_political_power
          >
          -1
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_depot_attempted
      }
      U25 = {
        add_political_power = -0
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_depot_attempted
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_depot
    set_variable = {
      wet_skv_offer_depot_days = 30
    }
    U25 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            is_in_array = {
              wet_skv_active_recipients = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        add_to_array = {
          wet_skv_active_recipients = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_skaven.112
    }
  }
}

wet_skv_resolve_karl = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_skv_op_karl
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        wet_skv_op_karl_valid = yes
      }
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_chance = 40
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_dossier
        }
        add_to_variable = {
          wet_skv_chance = 15
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          U25 = {
            has_country_flag = wet_skv_extraction_funded
          }
        }
        add_to_variable = {
          wet_skv_chance = 10
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          U25 = {
            has_idea = wet_skv_scapegoat_eshin
          }
        }
        subtract_from_variable = {
          wet_skv_chance = 10
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_op_karl_defended
        }
        subtract_from_variable = {
          wet_skv_chance = 25
        }
      }
      clamp_variable = {
        var = wet_skv_chance
        min = 10
        max = 85
      }
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_failure_chance = 100
      }
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_skv_failure_chance = wet_skv_chance
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_skv_op_karl
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_op_karl_days = 0
      }
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_op_karl_cooldown = 180
      }
      U25 = {
        subtract_from_variable = {
          wet_skv_major_active = 1
        }
        clamp_variable = {
          var = wet_skv_major_active
          min = 0
          max = 2
        }
      }
      random_list = {
        wet_skv_chance = {
          if = {
            limit = {
              has_country_leader = {
                character = WET_A01_leader
                ruling_only = yes
              }
            }
            kill_country_leader = yes
          }
          else = {
            retire_character = WET_A01_leader
          }
          add_stability = -0.1
          U25 = {
            if = {
              limit = {
                NOT = {
                  has_country_flag = wet_skv_success_karl
                }
              }
              set_country_flag = wet_skv_success_karl
              add_to_variable = {
                wet_skv_successful_operations = 1
              }
            }
            add_to_variable = {
              wet_skv_unpaid_spoils = 1
            }
          }
          U25 = {
            country_event = {
              id = wet_skaven.128
            }
          }
          set_country_flag = wet_skv_op_karl_won
        }
        wet_skv_failure_chance = {
          U25 = {
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
              type = support_equipment
              amount = 150
            }
            add_to_variable = {
              wet_skv_diverted = 75
            }
            country_event = {
              id = wet_skaven.129
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    else = {
      wet_skv_abort_karl = yes
    }
  }

      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_chance = 60
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_dossier
        }
        add_to_variable = {
          wet_skv_chance = 15
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          U25 = {
            has_country_flag = wet_skv_extraction_funded
          }
        }
        add_to_variable = {
          wet_skv_chance = 10
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          U25 = {
            has_idea = wet_skv_scapegoat_eshin
          }
        }
        subtract_from_variable = {
          wet_skv_chance = 10
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_op_supply_defended
        }
        subtract_from_variable = {
          wet_skv_chance = 25
        }
      }
      clamp_variable = {
        var = wet_skv_chance
        min = 10
        max = 85
      }
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_failure_chance = 100
      }
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_skv_failure_chance = wet_skv_chance
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_skv_op_supply
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_op_supply_days = 0
      }
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_op_supply_cooldown = 180
      }
      U25 = {
        subtract_from_variable = {
          wet_skv_major_active = 1
        }
        clamp_variable = {
          var = wet_skv_major_active
          min = 0
          max = 2
        }
      }
      random_list = {
        wet_skv_chance = {
          for_each_scope_loop = {
            array = wet_skv_op_supply_site
            add_dynamic_modifier = {
              modifier = wet_skv_supply
              scope = PREV
              days = 45
            }
          }
          U25 = {
            if = {
              limit = {
                NOT = {
                  has_country_flag = wet_skv_success_supply
                }
              }
              set_country_flag = wet_skv_success_supply
              add_to_variable = {
                wet_skv_successful_operations = 1
              }
            }
            add_to_variable = {
              wet_skv_unpaid_spoils = 1
            }
          }
          U25 = {
            country_event = {
              id = wet_skaven.152
            }
          }
        }
        wet_skv_failure_chance = {
          U25 = {
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
              type = support_equipment
              amount = 100
            }
            add_to_variable = {
              wet_skv_diverted = 50
            }
            country_event = {
              id = wet_skaven.153
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    else = {
      wet_skv_abort_supply = yes
    }
  }
}

      }

      }

            has_country_flag = wet_skv_extraction_funded
          }
        }

          wet_skv_chance = 10
        }
      }

          max = 2

wet_skv_destroy_registered_pyramid = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_skv_pyramid_owner = yes
    }
    clr_country_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_active
    clr_global_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_exists
    set_global_flag = wet_story_black_pyramid_destroyed
    remove_ideas = wet_skv_pyramid_power
    add_war_support = -0.1
    every_owned_state = {
      limit = {
        wet_skv_pyramid_site = yes
      }
      clr_state_flag = wet_skv_black_pyramid_site
      damage_building = {
        type = industrial_complex
        damage = 2
      }
    }
  }
}

wet_skv_reconcile_pyramid = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_global_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_exists
    }
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_active
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            wet_skv_pyramid_owner = yes
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_active
        clr_global_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_exists
        remove_ideas = wet_skv_pyramid_power
        every_state = {
          limit = {
            wet_skv_pyramid_site = yes
          }
          clr_state_flag = wet_skv_black_pyramid_site
        }
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_skv_world_project_disabled
          }
          NOT = {
            has_idea = wet_skv_pyramid_power
          }
        }
        add_ideas = wet_skv_pyramid_power
      }
    }
  }
}
wet_skv_pyramid_damage_838 = {

    limit = {
      838 = {
        wet_skv_pyramid_site = yes
      }
    }
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        wet_skv_pyramid_owner = yes
      }
      wet_skv_destroy_registered_pyramid = yes
    }
  }
}

      }
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_failure_chance = 100
      }
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_skv_failure_chance = wet_skv_chance
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_skv_op_counter
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_op_counter_days = 0
      }
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_op_counter_cooldown = 180
      }
      U25 = {
        subtract_from_variable = {
          wet_skv_major_active = 1
        }
        clamp_variable = {
          var = wet_skv_major_active
          min = 0
          max = 2
        }
      }
      random_list = {
        wet_skv_chance = {
          set_country_flag = {
            flag = wet_skv_world_project_disabled
            days = 90
          }
          if = {
            limit = {
              has_country_flag = wet_skv_pyramid_active
            }
            remove_ideas = wet_skv_pyramid_power
          }
          U25 = {
            if = {
              limit = {
                NOT = {
                  has_country_flag = wet_skv_success_counter
                }
              }
              set_country_flag = wet_skv_success_counter
              add_to_variable = {
                wet_skv_successful_operations = 1
              }
            }
            add_to_variable = {
              wet_skv_unpaid_spoils = 1
            }
          }
          U25 = {
            country_event = {
              id = wet_skaven.172
            }
          }
        }
        wet_skv_failure_chance = {
          U25 = {
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
              type = support_equipment
              amount = 250
            }
            add_to_variable = {
              wet_skv_diverted = 125
            }
            country_event = {
              id = wet_skaven.173
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    else = {
      wet_skv_abort_counter = yes
    }
  }
}

wet_skv_send_evacuation_aid = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      exists = yes
      wet_name_is_skaven = yes
      NOT = {
        tag = U25
      }
      NOT = {
        has_war_with = U25
      }
      wet_skv_covert_access = yes
      U25 = {
        has_equipment = {
          infantry_equipment
          >
          499
        }
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_evacuation_aid
      }
      check_variable = {
        wet_skv_offer_evacuation_aid_cooldown
        <
        1
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_evacuation_aid_accepted
      }
      OR = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_evacuation_aid_attempted
        }
        U25 = {
          has_political_power
          >
          -1
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_evacuation_aid_attempted
      }
      U25 = {
        add_political_power = -0
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_evacuation_aid_attempted
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_evacuation_aid
    set_variable = {
      wet_skv_offer_evacuation_aid_days = 30
    }
    U25 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            is_in_array = {
              wet_skv_active_recipients = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        add_to_array = {
          wet_skv_active_recipients = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_skaven.181
    }
  }
}

wet_skv_reconcile_benefits = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_skv_reward_F11
      any_other_country = {
        is_in_faction_with = U25
        any_enemy_country = {
          has_war_with = U25
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_idea = wet_skv_f11
        }
      }
      add_ideas = wet_skv_f11
    }
  }
  else = {
    remove_ideas = wet_skv_f11
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      OR = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_internal_moulder
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_internal_eshin
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_idea = wet_skv_internal_project_team
        }
      }
      add_ideas = wet_skv_internal_project_team
    }
  }
  else = {
    remove_ideas = wet_skv_internal_project_team
  }
}

wet_focus_actions_skaven = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_focus_running
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          AND = {
            capital_scope = {
              is_fully_controlled_by = U25
            }
          }
        }
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_focus_running
      set_variable = {
        wet_skv_shelters_focus_days = 0
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_active
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_active
        add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
          type = infantry_equipment
          amount = 250
        }
      }
    }
    else = {
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_skv_shelters_focus_days = 1
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_skv_shelters_focus_days
            <
            1
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_skv_shelters_focus_days = 0
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_active
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_active
          if = {
            limit = {
              capital_scope = {
                is_fully_controlled_by = U25
              }
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_ready
            add_timed_idea = {
              idea = wet_skv_shelters
              days = 365
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_done
          }
          else = {
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
              type = infantry_equipment
              amount = 250
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_focus_pending
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          always = no
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_focus_pending
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          has_completed_focus = WET_SKV_E06
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_done
          }
          has_equipment = {
            infantry_equipment
            >
            499
          }
          capital_scope = {
            is_fully_controlled_by = U25
          }
          has_political_power
          >
          149
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_focus_pending
        set_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_skv_shelters_focus_days = 180
        }
        add_political_power = -150
        set_country_flag = wet_skv_shelters_active
        add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
          type = infantry_equipment
          amount = -500
        }
      }
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_skv_relief_focus_running
    }
    if = {
      limit = {

            has_completed_focus = WET_SKV_F09
            event_target:wet_focus_recipient = {
              exists = yes
              is_in_faction_with = U25
              NOT = {
                has_war_with = U25
              }
              NOT = {
                has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_mediation
              }
              check_variable = {
                wet_skv_offer_mediation_cooldown
                <
                1
              }
              OR = {
                NOT = {
                  has_country_flag = wet_skv_offer_mediation_attempted
                }
                U25 = {
                  has_political_power
                  >
                  -1
                }
              }
            }
          }
          event_target:wet_focus_recipient = {
            wet_skv_send_mediation = yes
          }
        }
      }
    }
    wet_skv_reconcile_benefits = yes
  }

    set_country_flag = wet_skv_reward_C26
    add_stability = 0.25
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_scapegoat_pestilens
    add_timed_idea = {
      idea = wet_skv_scapegoat_pestilens
      days = 365
    }
    subtract_from_variable = {
      wet_skv_favour_pestilens = 30
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      wet_skv_rivalry = 15
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_skv_verdict_resolved
    wet_skv_reconcile_benefits = yes
  }
}
